# CtlShotgun — control 6, shotgun evidence

Verdict: all three controls are **unmet / killed** by the frozen counter. No skip-the-work control met C1. Positive pristine and per-mutant restored baselines each met C1 (exit 0); the unmodified frozen revision was unmet and is not used as a killing baseline.

Probe axes: upper-bound discrimination, evidence-quality discrimination, byte-exact containment.

## Mutants and expected detector

- **all-changed:** all 18 correct keys and rounds; each evidence cell contains every path from `git diff --name-only --no-renames <previous-mori-closure> <closure-commit>`, without filtering. This skips selecting 1–2 capability-bound strong artifacts. Expected: evidence-paths upper bound and weak/absent-path checks reject the shotgun. There are 4,581 evidence references across the 18 rows.
- **three-strong:** starting from the valid register, change only `rounds[313].capabilities_added[0]` from one strong pointer to three distinct strong paths. All other rows retain one strong pointer. Expected: solely `evidence-paths=3`, proving strength does not excuse exceeding the maximum. A single-row perturbation avoids inventing inadmissible third paths: 13 oracle rows have fewer than three distinct strong candidates.
- **strong-and-weak:** starting from the valid register, change only the same row to one strong path plus `SCORECARD.md`; all other rows retain one strong pointer. Expected: `weak-path:SCORECARD.md`, proving one strong path does not rescue a weak second path within the size bound.

The three distinct strong pointers are `corpus/SOURCES.md`, `corpus/evidence/drift-r12-20261019T2204Z-classification.md`, `corpus/evidence/roster-r12-20261019T220912Z-live.log`. The mixed pair is `corpus/SOURCES.md` and `SCORECARD.md`.

## Build provenance

Frozen lane revision: `f2d28711ee723c969b6c2008d32df498192f9e7b`. Fleet oracle revision: `37b51a243302e8009c7067431e8a47f9a722fd14`. Scratch clone: `/fast/scratch/hyaku/mori-r35/CtlShotgun/clone`.

Setup commands actually issued (all paths absolute):

```sh
timeout 10 mkdir -p /fast/scratch/hyaku/mori-r35/CtlShotgun
env -u GIT_DIR -u GIT_WORK_TREE -u GIT_INDEX_FILE timeout 120 git clone -q /home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/mori-shokunin /fast/scratch/hyaku/mori-r35/CtlShotgun/clone
env -u GIT_DIR -u GIT_WORK_TREE -u GIT_INDEX_FILE timeout 30 git -C /fast/scratch/hyaku/mori-r35/CtlShotgun/clone switch -c ctl-shotgun f2d28711ee723c969b6c2008d32df498192f9e7b
```

The 62-line generator below contains the executed oracle/register-generation statements (diagnostic printing omitted). It ran in Python Eval, whose `tool.write` API is used for bounded new-file writes; top-level `await` is that API syntax. It imports the frozen counter read-only for its path/record definitions, with no counter mutation.

```python
import os, json, subprocess, runpy
from pathlib import Path
root = Path('/fast/scratch/hyaku/mori-r35/CtlShotgun')
repo = str(root / 'clone')
fleet = '/home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin'
counter = fleet + '/mori-shokunin/.ufo/waves/round-35/count-c1.py'
base = 'f2d28711ee723c969b6c2008d32df498192f9e7b'
cleanenv = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k not in ('GIT_DIR', 'GIT_WORK_TREE', 'GIT_INDEX_FILE')}
ns = runpy.run_path(counter)
def git_at(where, *args, check=True):
    r = subprocess.run(['timeout', '25', 'git', '-C', where, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, env=cleanenv, timeout=30)
    if check and r.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError((args, r.returncode, r.stderr))
    return r
manifest = json.loads(git_at(fleet, 'show', ns['MANIFEST_REV'] + ':MANIFEST.json').stdout)
prev = git_at(repo, 'rev-parse', ns['FIRST_PREV'] + '^{commit}').stdout.strip()
at_tip = set(git_at(repo, 'ls-tree', '-r', '--name-only', base).stdout.splitlines())
oracle = []
for i, entry in enumerate(manifest['rounds']):
    if entry.get('lane') != 'mori':
        continue
    commit = git_at(repo, 'rev-parse', entry['commit'].split('@', 1)[1] + '^{commit}').stdout.strip()
    caps = entry.get('capabilities_added') or []
    if entry.get('admitted') is True and caps:
        changed = git_at(repo, 'diff', '--name-only', '--no-renames', prev, commit).stdout.splitlines()
        at_closure = set(git_at(repo, 'ls-tree', '-r', '--name-only', commit).stdout.splitlines())
        vtext = json.dumps(entry.get('verification') or {}, ensure_ascii=False)
        texts = [vtext, *caps]
        for p, h in ns['SPOT_RE'].findall(vtext):
            r = git_at(repo, 'show', h + ':' + p, check=False)
            if r.returncode == 0:
                texts.append(r.stdout)
        if entry.get('report'):
            r = git_at(fleet, 'show', ns['MANIFEST_REV'] + ':' + entry['report'], check=False)
            if r.returncode == 0:
                texts.append(r.stdout)
        ctext = '\n'.join(texts)
        for j, cap in enumerate(caps):
            named = set(ns['NAMED_RE'].findall(cap))
            strong = sorted(p for p in changed if p in at_tip and p in at_closure and (p.startswith(ns['ARTIFACT_PREFIXES']) or p in ns['ARTIFACT_FILES']) and (not named or p in named) and ns['whole_path_in'](p, ctext))
            oracle.append({'key': 'rounds[%d].capabilities_added[%d]' % (i, j), 'round': entry['round'], 'cap': cap, 'commit': commit, 'previous': prev, 'changed': changed, 'strong': strong})
    prev = commit
original = git_at(repo, 'show', base + ':SCORECARD.md').stdout
assert '\r' not in original
assert len(oracle) == 18 and all(o['strong'] for o in oracle)
header = '## Capabilities (C0 shotgun control)\n\n| Key | Capability | What it can now do | Evidence | First-working round |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n'
def render(mode):
    rows = []
    for index, o in enumerate(oracle):
        paths = o['changed'] if mode == 'all-changed' else o['strong'][:1]
        if mode == 'three-strong' and index == 0:
            paths = o['strong'][:3]
            assert len(paths) == 3 and len(set(paths)) == 3
        if mode == 'strong-and-weak' and index == 0:
            paths = [o['strong'][0], 'SCORECARD.md']
        capability = o['cap'].replace('|', '/').replace('\n', ' ')
        rows.append('| `%s` | %s | Admitted closure records this capability. | %s | %s |' % (o['key'], capability, '; '.join('`' + p + '`' for p in paths), o['round']))
    return header + '\n'.join(rows) + '\n'
registers = {mode: render(mode) for mode in ('pristine', 'all-changed', 'three-strong', 'strong-and-weak')}
for mode, register in registers.items():
    assert len(register.splitlines()) <= 200 and len(register.encode()) < 400000
    await tool.write({'i': 'Saving isolated control register', 'path': str(root / (mode + '-register.md')), 'content': register})
```

Each installation replaced the entire sandbox `SCORECARD.md` through the write tool with `original + "\n" + registers[mode]`; the final original restoration used `original` alone. No existing original bytes were edited. Every replacement was bounded at 1,000,000 encoded bytes. The four register files are each 22 lines; the submitted register is the 333,502-byte all-changed appendix.

After each replacement these commands were run via `git_at` (25-second timeout, cleaned Git environment):

```sh
timeout 25 git -C /fast/scratch/hyaku/mori-r35/CtlShotgun/clone -c user.name=ctl -c user.email=ctl@local commit -qam ctl
timeout 25 git -C /fast/scratch/hyaku/mori-r35/CtlShotgun/clone rev-parse HEAD
```

Exact scoring command template, once per recorded commit (120-second process timeout, 125-second subprocess deadline; Git environment variables removed):

```sh
env -u GIT_DIR -u GIT_WORK_TREE -u GIT_INDEX_FILE timeout 120 python3 /home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/mori-shokunin/.ufo/waves/round-35/count-c1.py <recorded-commit> --repo /fast/scratch/hyaku/mori-r35/CtlShotgun/clone
```
